+// src/index.ts
+/**
+ * @fileoverview Main entry point for the AutoResearch CLI.
+ * Handles command parsing, user prompts, history, and orchestrates the research engine.
+ */
+import * as p from "@clack/prompts";
+import { Command } from "commander";
+import pc from "picocolors";
+import { z } from "zod";
+
+/**
+ * Commander program instance for the AutoResearch CLI.
+ */
+const program = new Command();
+
+/**
+ * Zod schema for validating research input parameters.
+ * Ensures topic is within length limits and depth is an integer between 1 and 5.
+ */
+const ResearchInputSchema = z.object({
+	topic: z
+		.string()
+		.trim()
+		.min(1, "Topic cannot be empty")
+		.max(200, "Topic is too long"),
+	depth: z.coerce
+		.number()
+		.int()
+		.min(1, "Depth must be at least 1")
+		.max(5, "Depth cannot exceed 5"),
+});
+
+program
+	.name("autoresearch")
+	.description("Enterprise AI Autonomous Research framework")
+	.version("1.0.41");
+
+/**
+ * Auth command action.
+ * Prompts the user for API keys and endpoint settings, saving them securely via ConfigManager.
+ */
+program
+	.command("auth")
+	.description("Configure OpenAI, Serper, and custom model endpoints securely.")
+	.action(async () => {
+		const { ConfigManager } = await import("./utils/config");
+		const configManager = new ConfigManager();
+
+		p.intro(pc.bgBlue(pc.white(" AutoResearch Configuration ")));
+
+		const openAiKey = await p.password({
+			message: "Enter your OpenAI API key (or local/proxy token):",
+			validate: (value) => {
+				if (!value) return "API key cannot be empty.";
+			},
+		});
+
+		if (p.isCancel(openAiKey)) {
+			p.cancel("Operation cancelled.");
+			process.exit(0);
+		}
+
+		const customBaseUrl = await p.text({
+			message:
+				"Custom Base URL (Optional - for Ollama, vLLM, LMStudio, OpenRouter, DeepSeek):",
+			placeholder:
+				"e.g., http://localhost:11434/v1 or https://openrouter.ai/api/v1",
+		});
+
+		if (p.isCancel(customBaseUrl)) {
+			p.cancel("Operation cancelled.");
+			process.exit(0);
+		}
+
+		const customModel = await p.text({
+			message: "LLM Model Name (Optional, defaults to gpt-4-turbo-preview):",
+			placeholder: "e.g., gpt-4o, deepseek-chat, mistral-large",
+		});
+
+		if (p.isCancel(customModel)) {
+			p.cancel("Operation cancelled.");
+			process.exit(0);
+		}
+
+		const serperKey = await p.password({
+			message:
+				"Enter Serper API key (Optional - falls back to built-in DuckDuckGo search if left blank):",
+		});
+
+		if (p.isCancel(serperKey)) {
+			p.cancel("Operation cancelled.");
+			process.exit(0);
+		}
+
+		const s = p.spinner();
+		s.start("Saving configuration securely...");
+		try {
+			await configManager.setConfig({
+				OPENAI_API_KEY: openAiKey as string,
+				OPENAI_BASE_URL:
+					typeof customBaseUrl === "string" && customBaseUrl.trim()
+						? customBaseUrl.trim()
+						: undefined,
+				OPENAI_MODEL:
+					typeof customModel === "string" && customModel.trim()
+						? customModel.trim()
+						: undefined,
+				SERPER_API_KEY:
+					typeof serperKey === "string" && serperKey.trim()
+						? serperKey.trim()
+						: undefined,
+			});
+			s.stop(
+				pc.green("Configuration saved securely to ~/.autoresearch/config.json"),
+			);
+
+			p.outro(pc.cyan("You're ready to research!"));
+		} catch (error: unknown) {
+			s.stop(pc.red("Failed to save configuration."));
+			p.log.error(
+				pc.red(error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error)),
+			);
+			process.exit(1);
+		}
+	});
+
+/**
+ * Research command action.
+ * Collects topic and depth via CLI flags or interactive prompts, validates them,
+ * executes the research pipeline, and exports structured artifacts.
+ */
+program
+	.command("research")
+	.description("Run an autonomous deep-dive research on a topic.")
+	.option("-t, --topic <string>", "The topic to research")
+	.option("-d, --depth <number>", "Research depth (1-5)")
+	.option("-o, --output <dir>", "Directory to export reports", "./reports")
+	.option(
+		"-f, --format <format>",
+		"Output formats to generate: all, markdown, html, json",
+		"all",
+	)
+	.option("--no-save", "Do not write files to disk, output to terminal only")
+	.action(async (options) => {
+		p.intro(pc.bgBlue(pc.white(" AutoResearch Engine ")));
+
+		// Interactive Prompts if not provided via flags
+		let topic = options.topic;
+		if (!topic) {
+			topic = await p.text({
+				message: "What topic would you like to research?",
+				placeholder: "e.g., Quantum Computing advancements in 2026",
+				validate: (value) => {
+					if (!value || typeof value !== "string" || !value.trim())
+						return "Topic cannot be empty";
+					if (value.length > 200)
+						return "Topic is too long (max 200 characters).";
+				},
+			});
+			if (p.isCancel(topic)) {
+				p.cancel("Operation cancelled.");
+				process.exit(0);
+			}
+		}
+
+		let depth = options.depth ? Number.parseInt(options.depth, 10) : null;
+		if (!depth) {
+			const depthSelection = await p.select({
+				message: "Select research depth (1=Surface, 5=Deep Analysis):",
+				options: [
+					{ value: 1, label: "1 - Quick Overview" },
+					{ value: 2, label: "2 - Basic Summary" },
+					{ value: 3, label: "3 - Standard Analysis" },
+					{ value: 4, label: "4 - Comprehensive Review" },
+					{ value: 5, label: "5 - Deep Dive" },
+				],
+			});
+			if (p.isCancel(depthSelection)) {
+				p.cancel("Operation cancelled.");
+				process.exit(0);
+			}
+			depth = depthSelection as number;
+		}
+
+		// Validate inputs against schema
+		const parseResult = ResearchInputSchema.safeParse({ topic, depth });
+		if (!parseResult.success) {
+			p.log.error(pc.red("Invalid input parameters."));
+			for (const issue of parseResult.error.issues) {
+				p.log.error(pc.red(` - ${issue.path.join(".")}: ${issue.message}`));
+			}
+			process.exit(1);
+		}
+
+		const s = p.spinner();
+		s.start("Initializing research engine...");
+
+		try {
+			const { ResearchEngine } = await import("./core/engine");
+			const engine = new ResearchEngine({ depth: parseResult.data.depth });
+
+			s.stop(pc.green("Research engine initialized."));
+			p.log.step(
+				pc.cyan(
+					`Starting research on: "${pc.bold(parseResult.data.topic)}" (Depth: ${parseResult.data.depth})`,
+				),
+			);
+
+			s.start("Conducting research...");
+			const reportMarkdown = await engine.run(
+				parseResult.data.topic,
+				(status) => {
+					s.message(status);
+				},
+			);
+			s.stop(pc.green("Research completed."));
+
+			// Print the generated report to stdout
+			console.log(`\n${reportMarkdown}\n`);
+
+			// Export artifacts and record in history if save is enabled
+			if (options.save !== false) {
+				const reportData =
+					typeof engine.getLastReport === "function"
+						? engine.getLastReport()
+						: null;
+
+				if (reportData && reportData.sources.length > 0) {
+					const { exportReport } = await import("./exporters");
+					const { HistoryManager } = await import("./storage/history");
+
+					const rawFormat = (options.format || "all").toLowerCase();
+					let exportFormats: ("markdown" | "html" | "json")[] = [
+						"markdown",
+						"html",
+						"json",
+					];
+					if (rawFormat === "markdown" || rawFormat === "md") {
+						exportFormats = ["markdown"];
+					} else if (rawFormat === "html") {
+						exportFormats = ["html"];
+					} else if (rawFormat === "json") {
+						exportFormats = ["json"];
+					}
+
+					const outputDir = options.output || "./reports";
+					const exportedFiles = await exportReport(
+						reportData,
+						outputDir,
+						exportFormats,
+					);
+
+					p.log.message(pc.bold(pc.cyan("📁 Generated Artifacts:")));
+					const fileMap: { markdown?: string; html?: string; json?: string } =
+						{};
+					for (const file of exportedFiles) {
+						fileMap[file.format] = file.filePath;
+						p.log.message(
+							`  ${pc.green("✓")} ${pc.white(file.filename)}: ${pc.gray(file.filePath)}`,
+						);
+					}
+
+					// Save run to local history library
+					const historyManager = new HistoryManager();
+					await historyManager.saveRun({
+						id: reportData.metadata.id,
+						slug: reportData.metadata.slug,
+						topic: reportData.metadata.topic,
+						depth: reportData.metadata.depth,
+						model: reportData.metadata.model,
+						createdAt: reportData.metadata.createdAt,
+						sourcesCount: reportData.metadata.sourcesCount,
+						subQueriesCount: reportData.subQueries.length,
+						outputDir,
+						files: fileMap,
+					});
+				}
+			}
+
+			p.outro(pc.green("Research completed successfully!"));
+		} catch (error: unknown) {
+			s.stop(pc.red("Research failed."));
+			p.log.error(
+				pc.red(error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error)),
+			);
+			process.exit(1);
+		}
+	});
+
+/**
+ * List command action.
+ * Displays past research reports stored in ~/.autoresearch/history.json.
+ */
+program
+	.command("list")
+	.description("List past research runs stored in your local research library.")
+	.option("-n, --limit <number>", "Number of records to display", "15")
+	.action(async (options) => {
+		const { HistoryManager } = await import("./storage/history");
+		const history = new HistoryManager();
+		const limit = Number.parseInt(options.limit, 10) || 15;
+		const runs = await history.listRuns(limit);
+
+		if (runs.length === 0) {
+			console.log(pc.yellow("No past research runs found in history."));
+			console.log(
+				pc.gray(
+					"Run `autoresearch research` to start your first research session.",
+				),
+			);
+			return;
+		}
+
+		console.log(
+			pc.bold(pc.cyan(`\n📚 Research History (${runs.length} runs):\n`)),
+		);
+		console.log(
+			pc.bold(
+				`${"ID".padEnd(16)} ${"DATE".padEnd(20)} ${"DEPTH".padEnd(7)} ${"SOURCES".padEnd(9)} TOPIC`,
+			),
+		);
+		console.log(pc.gray("-".repeat(80)));
+
+		for (const r of runs) {
+			const idStr = r.id.padEnd(16);
+			const dateStr = new Date(r.createdAt).toLocaleDateString().padEnd(20);
+			const depthStr = String(r.depth).padEnd(7);
+			const srcStr = String(r.sourcesCount).padEnd(9);
+			const topicStr =
+				r.topic.length > 40 ? `${r.topic.slice(0, 37)}...` : r.topic;
+
+			console.log(
+				`${pc.cyan(idStr)} ${dateStr} ${depthStr} ${srcStr} ${pc.white(topicStr)}`,
+			);
+		}
+		console.log();
+	});
+
+/**
+ * View command action.
+ * Views or opens a past research report by ID or slug.
+ */
+program
+	.command("view <idOrSlug>")
+	.description("View or open a past research report.")
+	.option("--open", "Open the HTML report in default web browser")
+	.action(async (idOrSlug, options) => {
+		const { HistoryManager } = await import("./storage/history");
+		const history = new HistoryManager();
+		const entry = await history.getRun(idOrSlug);
+
+		if (!entry) {
+			console.error(
+				pc.red(`Error: No research run found matching "${idOrSlug}".`),
+			);
+			console.log(
+				pc.gray("Run `autoresearch list` to see available research IDs."),
+			);
+			process.exit(1);
+		}
+
+		if (options.open && entry.files.html) {
+			const { exec } = await import("node:child_process");
+			const cmd =
+				process.platform === "win32"
+					? `start "" "${entry.files.html}"`
+					: process.platform === "darwin"
+						? `open "${entry.files.html}"`
+						: `xdg-open "${entry.files.html}"`;
+			exec(cmd);
+			console.log(pc.green(`Opened ${entry.files.html} in browser.`));
+			return;
+		}
+
+		// Read and print markdown report
+		if (entry.files.markdown) {
+			const fs = await import("node:fs/promises");
+			try {
+				const md = await fs.readFile(entry.files.markdown, "utf-8");
+				console.log(`\n${md}\n`);
+				return;
+			} catch {}
+		}
+
+		console.log(pc.bold(pc.cyan(`\nResearch Run: ${entry.topic}`)));
+		console.log(`ID: ${entry.id}`);
+		console.log(`Created: ${new Date(entry.createdAt).toLocaleString()}`);
+		console.log(`Depth: ${entry.depth} | Sources: ${entry.sourcesCount}`);
+		if (entry.files.html) console.log(`HTML Report: ${entry.files.html}`);
+		if (entry.files.markdown)
+			console.log(`Markdown Report: ${entry.files.markdown}`);
+	});
+
+export const cliPromise = program.parseAsync();

+import * as fs from "node:fs/promises";
+import * as os from "node:os";
+import * as path from "node:path";
+import type { ReportData } from "../exporters/types";
+
+export interface HistoryEntry {
+	id: string;
+	slug: string;
+	topic: string;
+	depth: number;
+	model: string;
+	createdAt: string;
+	sourcesCount: number;
+	subQueriesCount: number;
+	outputDir?: string;
+	files: {
+		markdown?: string;
+		html?: string;
+		json?: string;
+	};
+}
+
+export class HistoryManager {
+	private readonly historyPath: string;
+	private readonly historyDir: string;
+
+	constructor(customPathOrDir?: string) {
+		if (customPathOrDir) {
+			if (customPathOrDir.endsWith(".json")) {
+				this.historyPath = path.resolve(customPathOrDir);
+				this.historyDir = path.dirname(this.historyPath);
+			} else {
+				this.historyDir = path.resolve(customPathOrDir);
+				this.historyPath = path.join(this.historyDir, "history.json");
+			}
+		} else {
+			this.historyDir = path.join(os.homedir(), ".autoresearch");
+			this.historyPath = path.join(this.historyDir, "history.json");
+		}
+	}
+
+	private async readAll(): Promise<HistoryEntry[]> {
+		try {
+			const data = await fs.readFile(this.historyPath, "utf-8");
+			const parsed = JSON.parse(data);
+			return Array.isArray(parsed) ? parsed : [];
+		} catch {
+			return [];
+		}
+	}
+
+	private async writeAll(entries: HistoryEntry[]): Promise<void> {
+		await fs.mkdir(this.historyDir, { recursive: true });
+		await fs.writeFile(this.historyPath, JSON.stringify(entries, null, 2), {
+			encoding: "utf-8",
+			mode: 0o600,
+		});
+	}
+
+	public async saveRun(
+		entryOrReport: HistoryEntry | ReportData,
+		files: { markdown?: string; html?: string; json?: string } = {},
+	): Promise<HistoryEntry> {
+		const entry: HistoryEntry =
+			"metadata" in entryOrReport
+				? {
+						id: entryOrReport.metadata.id,
+						slug: entryOrReport.metadata.slug,
+						topic: entryOrReport.metadata.topic,
+						depth: entryOrReport.metadata.depth,
+						model: entryOrReport.metadata.model,
+						createdAt: entryOrReport.metadata.createdAt,
+						sourcesCount: entryOrReport.metadata.sourcesCount,
+						subQueriesCount: entryOrReport.subQueries?.length || 0,
+						files: files || {},
+					}
+				: {
+						...entryOrReport,
+						files: { ...(entryOrReport.files || {}), ...(files || {}) },
+					};
+
+		const entries = await this.readAll();
+		// Prepend so latest runs are at index 0
+		const updated = [
+			entry,
+			...entries.filter((e) => e.id !== entry.id && e.slug !== entry.slug),
+		];
+		// Keep up to 100 recent entries
+		await this.writeAll(updated.slice(0, 100));
+		return entry;
+	}
+
+	public async listRuns(limit = 20): Promise<HistoryEntry[]> {
+		const entries = await this.readAll();
+		return entries.slice(0, limit);
+	}
+
+	public async getRun(idOrSlug: string): Promise<HistoryEntry | null> {
+		const entries = await this.readAll();
+		const query = idOrSlug.toLowerCase();
+		const match = entries.find(
+			(e) =>
+				(e.id || "").toLowerCase() === query ||
+				(e.slug || "").toLowerCase() === query,
+		);
+		return match || null;
+	}
+
+	public async deleteRun(idOrSlug: string): Promise<boolean> {
+		const entries = await this.readAll();
+		const initialLength = entries.length;
+		const query = idOrSlug.toLowerCase();
+		const filtered = entries.filter(
+			(e) =>
+				(e.id || "").toLowerCase() !== query &&
+				(e.slug || "").toLowerCase() !== query,
+		);
+		if (filtered.length !== initialLength) {
+			await this.writeAll(filtered);
+			return true;
+		}
+		return false;
+	}
+
+	public async clearAll(): Promise<void> {
+		await this.writeAll([]);
+	}
+
+	public async clear(): Promise<void> {
+		await this.clearAll();
+	}
+}

+import type { ISearchProvider, SearchResult } from "./search/types";
+
+function decodeBasicEntities(text: string): string {
+	return text
+		.replace(/&amp;/g, "&")
+		.replace(/&lt;/g, "<")
+		.replace(/&gt;/g, ">")
+		.replace(/&quot;/g, '"')
+		.replace(/&#39;/g, "'")
+		.replace(/&apos;/g, "'")
+		.replace(/&nbsp;/g, " ")
+		.replace(/\s+/g, " ")
+		.trim();
+}
+
+function cleanHtml(text: string): string {
+	return decodeBasicEntities(text.replace(/<[^>]+>/g, " "));
+}
+
+export class DuckDuckGoSearcher implements ISearchProvider {
+	public readonly name = "DuckDuckGo";
+	private cache: Map<string, Promise<SearchResult[]>> = new Map();
+
+	constructor(private maxRetries = 2) {}
+
+	public async search(query: string, numResults = 5): Promise<SearchResult[]> {
+		const trimmedQuery = query.trim();
+		if (!trimmedQuery) {
+			return [];
+		}
+
+		const cacheKey = `${trimmedQuery}:${numResults}`;
+		if (this.cache.has(cacheKey)) {
+			return this.cache.get(cacheKey) as Promise<SearchResult[]>;
+		}
+
+		const searchPromise = (async () => {
+			let attempt = 0;
+			while (attempt <= this.maxRetries) {
+				try {
+					const encoded = encodeURIComponent(trimmedQuery);
+					const url = `https://html.duckduckgo.com/html/?q=${encoded}`;
+
+					const response = await fetch(url, {
+						headers: {
+							"User-Agent":
+								"Mozilla/5.0 (Windows NT 10.0; Win64; x64) AppleWebKit/537.36 (KHTML, like Gecko) Chrome/126.0.0.0 Safari/537.36",
+							Accept:
+								"text/html,application/xhtml+xml,application/xml;q=0.9,*/*;q=0.8",
+							"Accept-Language": "en-US,en;q=0.9",
+						},
+						signal: AbortSignal.timeout(10000),
+					});
+
+					if (!response.ok) {
+						throw new Error(`DuckDuckGo HTTP error: ${response.status}`);
+					}
+
+					const html = await response.text();
+					const results: SearchResult[] = [];
+
+					// Parse title links and snippet blocks
+					// Pattern: <a class="result__a"[^>]*href="([^"]+)"[^>]*>([\s\S]*?)<\/a>
+					const titleRegex =
+						/<a[^>]+class="[^"]*result__a[^"]*"[^>]+href="([^"]+)"[^>]*>([\s\S]*?)<\/a>/gi;
+					const snippetRegex =
+						/<a[^>]+class="[^"]*result__snippet[^"]*"[^>]*>([\s\S]*?)<\/a>/gi;
+
+					const titles: { href: string; title: string }[] = [];
+					let titleMatch: RegExpExecArray | null = titleRegex.exec(html);
+					while (titleMatch !== null) {
+						titles.push({
+							href: titleMatch[1],
+							title: cleanHtml(titleMatch[2]),
+						});
+						titleMatch = titleRegex.exec(html);
+					}
+
+					const snippets: string[] = [];
+					let snippetMatch: RegExpExecArray | null = snippetRegex.exec(html);
+					while (snippetMatch !== null) {
+						snippets.push(cleanHtml(snippetMatch[1]));
+						snippetMatch = snippetRegex.exec(html);
+					}
+
+					for (
+						let i = 0;
+						i < titles.length && results.length < numResults;
+						i++
+					) {
+						let rawLink = titles[i].href;
+
+						// Extract target link from DuckDuckGo redirect wrapper: /l/?uddg=<url>&...
+						if (rawLink.includes("uddg=")) {
+							const uddgMatch = rawLink.match(/uddg=([^&]+)/);
+							if (uddgMatch) {
+								try {
+									rawLink = decodeURIComponent(uddgMatch[1]);
+								} catch {}
+							}
+						}
+
+						if (rawLink.startsWith("//")) {
+							rawLink = `https:${rawLink}`;
+						}
+
+						// Validate URL format
+						try {
+							const parsedUrl = new URL(rawLink);
+							if (
+								parsedUrl.protocol === "http:" ||
+								parsedUrl.protocol === "https:"
+							) {
+								results.push({
+									title: titles[i].title || "Untitled",
+									link: parsedUrl.toString(),
+									snippet: snippets[i] || "",
+								});
+							}
+						} catch {}
+					}
+
+					return results;
+				} catch (error) {
+					attempt++;
+					if (attempt > this.maxRetries) {
+						this.cache.delete(cacheKey);
+						return [];
+					}
+					await new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, attempt * 1000));
+				}
+			}
+
+			return [];
+		})();
+
+		this.cache.set(cacheKey, searchPromise);
+		return searchPromise;
+	}
+}
diff --git a/src/tools/GoogleSearcher.ts b/src/tools/GoogleSearcher.ts
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..98f28bb
--- /dev/null
+++ b/src/tools/GoogleSearcher.ts
@@ -0,0 +1,161 @@
+import * as p from "@clack/prompts";
+import pc from "picocolors";
+import { ConfigManager } from "../utils/config";
+import type { ISearchProvider, SearchResult } from "./search/types";
+
+class HttpError extends Error {
+	constructor(
+		message: string,
+		public status: number,
+	) {
+		super(message);
+		this.name = "HttpError";
+	}
+}
+
+export class GoogleSearcher implements ISearchProvider {
+	public readonly name = "GoogleSerper";
+	private configManager: ConfigManager;
+	private cache: Map<string, Promise<SearchResult[]>>;
+
+	constructor(configManager?: ConfigManager) {
+		this.configManager = configManager || new ConfigManager();
+		this.cache = new Map();
+	}
+
+	/**
+	 * Executes an API call with exponential backoff and retries.
+	 */
+	private async withRetries<T>(
+		operation: () => Promise<T>,
+		maxRetries = 3,
+	): Promise<T> {
+		let attempt = 0;
+		while (attempt < maxRetries) {
+			try {
+				return await operation();
+			} catch (error: unknown) {
+				attempt++;
+
+				if (
+					error instanceof HttpError &&
+					error.status >= 400 &&
+					error.status < 500 &&
+					error.status !== 429
+				) {
+					throw error; // Fail fast on client errors (e.g., 401, 403, 404)
+				}
+
+				if (attempt === maxRetries) {
+					console.error(
+						`[GoogleSearcher] Max retries exceeded. Error: ${error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error)}`,
+					);
+					throw error;
+				}
+
+				console.warn(
+					`[GoogleSearcher] Attempt ${attempt} failed. Retrying in ${2 ** (attempt - 1)}s... Error: ${error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error)}`,
+				);
+
+				// Exponential backoff: 1s, 2s, 4s
+				const delayMs = 2 ** (attempt - 1) * 1000;
+				await new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, delayMs));
+			}
+		}
+		throw new Error("Max retries exceeded.");
+	}
+
+	/**
+	 * Performs a Google search using Serper.dev API.
+	 */
+	public async search(query: string, numResults = 5): Promise<SearchResult[]> {
+		const trimmedQuery = query.trim();
+		if (!trimmedQuery) {
+			return [];
+		}
+
+		const cacheKey = `${trimmedQuery}:${numResults}`;
+		if (this.cache.has(cacheKey)) {
+			return this.cache.get(cacheKey) as Promise<SearchResult[]>;
+		}
+
+		const searchPromise = (async () => {
+			try {
+				const apiKey = await this.configManager.get("SERPER_API_KEY");
+
+				if (!apiKey) {
+					p.log.warn(
+						pc.yellow(
+							"[Search] SERPER_API_KEY not found. Skipping web search phase.",
+						),
+					);
+					return [];
+				}
+
+				return await this.withRetries(async () => {
+					const response = await fetch("https://google.serper.dev/search", {
+						method: "POST",
+						headers: {
+							"X-API-KEY": apiKey,
+							"Content-Type": "application/json",
+						},
+						body: JSON.stringify({
+							q: query,
+							num: numResults,
+						}),
+						signal: AbortSignal.timeout(10000),
+					});
+
+					if (!response.ok) {
+						try {
+							await response.body?.cancel();
+						} catch (err) {}
+
+						throw new HttpError(
+							`Serper API HTTP error: ${response.status} - ${response.statusText}`,
+							response.status,
+						);
+					}
+
+					const data = await response.json();
+
+					if (!data.organic || !Array.isArray(data.organic)) {
+						return [];
+					}
+
+					return data.organic
+						.filter(
+							(item: Record<string, unknown>) =>
+								Boolean(item) &&
+								typeof item.link === "string" &&
+								item.link.trim() !== "",
+						)
+						.map((item: Record<string, unknown>) => ({
+							title:
+								typeof item.title === "string"
+									? item.title
+									: item.title != null
+										? String(item.title)
+										: "",
+							link: String(item.link).trim(),
+							snippet:
+								typeof item.snippet === "string"
+									? item.snippet
+									: item.snippet != null
+										? String(item.snippet)
+										: "",
+						}));
+				});
+			} catch (error) {
+				console.error(
+					`[GoogleSearcher] Search failed for query "${query}": ${error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error)}`,
+				);
+				this.cache.delete(cacheKey);
+				throw error;
+			}
+		})();
+
+		this.cache.set(cacheKey, searchPromise);
+		return searchPromise;
+	}
+}
diff --git a/src/tools/SearchManager.ts b/src/tools/SearchManager.ts
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..1481382
--- /dev/null
+++ b/src/tools/SearchManager.ts
@@ -0,0 +1,78 @@
+import { ConfigManager } from "../utils/config";
+import { DuckDuckGoSearcher } from "./DuckDuckGoSearcher";
+import { GoogleSearcher } from "./GoogleSearcher";
+import type { ISearchProvider, SearchResult } from "./search/types";
+
+export class SearchManager implements ISearchProvider {
+	public readonly name = "SearchManager";
+	private configManager: ConfigManager;
+	private googleSearcher: GoogleSearcher;
+	private ddgSearcher: DuckDuckGoSearcher;
+
+	constructor(configManager?: ConfigManager) {
+		this.configManager = configManager || new ConfigManager();
+		this.googleSearcher = new GoogleSearcher(this.configManager);
+		this.ddgSearcher = new DuckDuckGoSearcher();
+	}
+
+	public async search(query: string, numResults = 5): Promise<SearchResult[]> {
+		const trimmed = query.trim();
+		if (!trimmed) return [];
+
+		const serperKey = await this.configManager.get("SERPER_API_KEY");
+
+		if (serperKey) {
+			try {
+				const results = await this.googleSearcher.search(trimmed, numResults);
+				if (results.length > 0) {
+					return results;
+				}
+			} catch (err) {
+				// Fallback to DuckDuckGo if Serper fails or rate-limits
+			}
+		}
+
+		// Fallback to zero-config DuckDuckGo provider
+		return await this.ddgSearcher.search(trimmed, numResults);
+	}
+
+	/**
+	 * Executes multiple investigative search queries concurrently,
+	 * deduplicating the aggregated results across queries by normalized URL.
+	 */
+	public async searchMulti(
+		queries: string[],
+		numPerQuery = 4,
+	): Promise<SearchResult[]> {
+		const uniqueQueries = Array.from(
+			new Set(queries.map((q) => q.trim()).filter(Boolean)),
+		);
+		if (uniqueQueries.length === 0) return [];
+
+		const searchPromises = uniqueQueries.map((q) =>
+			this.search(q, numPerQuery),
+		);
+		const nestedResults = await Promise.all(searchPromises);
+
+		const seenUrls = new Set<string>();
+		const aggregated: SearchResult[] = [];
+
+		for (const results of nestedResults) {
+			for (const r of results) {
+				let normalized = r.link;
+				try {
+					const parsed = new URL(r.link);
+					parsed.hash = "";
+					normalized = parsed.toString();
+				} catch {}
+
+				if (!seenUrls.has(normalized)) {
+					seenUrls.add(normalized);
+					aggregated.push(r);
+				}
+			}
+		}
+
+		return aggregated;
+	}
+}
diff --git a/src/tools/WebFetcher.ts b/src/tools/WebFetcher.ts
new file mode 100644

+++ b/src/tools/WebFetcher.ts
@@ -0,0 +1,383 @@
+import * as dns from "node:dns/promises";
+import * as url from "node:url";
+import * as ipaddr from "ipaddr.js";
+import pLimit from "p-limit";
+import { Agent, fetch as undiciFetch } from "undici";
+import { extractCharset } from "../utils/http";
+
+export class WebFetcher {
+	/**
+	 * Cache intentionally uses `normalizedUrl` (target URL with the hash fragment removed) as its key.
+	 * This focuses on caching the resource itself for idempotent GETs, preventing redundant network
+	 * requests if multiple sections of the same page (using different #hashes) are requested concurrently.
+	 */
+	private cache: Map<string, Promise<string>>;
+	private hostValidationCache: Map<string, Promise<string | null>>;
+	private maxConcurrency: number;
+	private dispatcher?: Agent;
+
+	constructor(maxConcurrency = 3, dispatcher?: Agent) {
+		this.cache = new Map();
+		this.hostValidationCache = new Map();
+		this.maxConcurrency = maxConcurrency;
+		this.dispatcher = dispatcher;
+	}
+
+	/**
+	 * Validates a URL to ensure it is safe (SSRF protection).
+	 * Only allows http and https protocols.
+	 * Performs DNS resolution to prevent DNS rebinding and loopback variants.
+	 */
+	private async isValidUrl(targetUrl: string): Promise<string | null> {
+		try {
+			const parsed = new url.URL(targetUrl);
+
+			// Rejects file://, ftp://, gopher://, etc.
+			if (parsed.protocol !== "http:" && parsed.protocol !== "https:") {
+				return null;
+			}
+
+			let hostname = parsed.hostname;
+
+			// dns.lookup does not support bracketed IPv6 literals, so we must strip them.
+			if (hostname.startsWith("[") && hostname.endsWith("]")) {
+				hostname = hostname.slice(1, -1);
+			}
+
+			if (this.hostValidationCache.has(hostname)) {
+				return this.hostValidationCache.get(hostname) as Promise<string | null>;
+			}
+
+			const validationPromise = (async () => {
+				// Resolve the hostname to prevent DNS rebinding or obfuscated IP representations
+				let addresses: { address: string; family: number }[];
+				try {
+					addresses = await dns.lookup(hostname, { all: true });
+				} catch {
+					// If DNS lookup fails (e.g., domain doesn't exist), we can't fetch it anyway
+					return null;
+				}
+
+				if (!addresses || addresses.length === 0) {
+					return null;
+				}
+
+				for (const { address } of addresses) {
+					try {
+						const parsedIp = ipaddr.parse(address);
+						let range = parsedIp.range();
+
+						// Handle IPv4-mapped IPv6 addresses specifically
+						if (
+							parsedIp.kind() === "ipv6" &&
+							(parsedIp as ipaddr.IPv6).isIPv4MappedAddress()
+						) {
+							range = (parsedIp as ipaddr.IPv6).toIPv4Address().range();
+						}
+
+						// Strict whitelist: Only allow regular public unicast addresses.
+						// This implicitly rejects private, loopback, multicast, broadcast, and metadata ranges.
+						if (range === "unicast") {
+							return address;
+						}
+					} catch {
+						// Invalid IP format
+						return null;
+					}
+				}
+
+				return null;
+			})();
+
+			this.hostValidationCache.set(hostname, validationPromise);
+
+			// Prevent DNS rebinding by only caching in-flight requests (request coalescing)
+			validationPromise
+				.finally(() => {
+					if (this.hostValidationCache.get(hostname) === validationPromise) {
+						this.hostValidationCache.delete(hostname);
+					}
+				})
+				.catch(() => {});
+
+			return validationPromise;
+		} catch {
+			return null;
+		}
+	}
+
+	/**
+	 * Internal generic fetcher handling a single URL with error catching.
+	 */
+	private fetchSingle(targetUrl: string): Promise<string> {
+		// Normalize the target URL early to avoid redundant network requests and duplicate cache entries
+		let normalizedUrl = targetUrl;
+		try {
+			const parsed = new URL(targetUrl);
+			parsed.hash = "";
+			normalizedUrl = parsed.toString();
+		} catch {}
+
+		if (this.cache.has(normalizedUrl)) {
+			return this.cache.get(normalizedUrl) as Promise<string>;
+		}
+
+		const fetchPromise = (async () => {
+			let currentUrl = targetUrl;
+			let safeIp = await this.isValidUrl(normalizedUrl);
+
+			if (!safeIp) {
+				this.cache.delete(normalizedUrl);
+
+				return `Error: Invalid or insecure URL provided (${targetUrl})`;
+			}
+
+			let response: Response | null = null;
+			let reader: ReadableStreamDefaultReader<Uint8Array> | null = null;
+			const createdDispatchers: Agent[] = [];
+			try {
+				let redirects = 0;
+				const MAX_REDIRECTS = 5;
+				const abortSignal = AbortSignal.timeout(15000);
+
+				while (redirects <= MAX_REDIRECTS) {
+					const family = safeIp.includes(":") ? 6 : 4;
+					// Use the injected dispatcher or construct one that binds the hostname to safeIp
+					// We only close the dispatcher if we constructed it ourselves
+					const dispatcher =
+						this.dispatcher ||
+						new Agent({
+							connect: {
+								lookup: (hostname, options, callback) => {
+									callback(null, [{ address: safeIp as string, family }]);
+								},
+							},
+						});
+
+					if (!this.dispatcher) {
+						createdDispatchers.push(dispatcher);
+					}
+
+					response = (await undiciFetch(currentUrl, {
+						headers: { "User-Agent": "AutoResearchAgent/2.0" },
+						signal: abortSignal,
+						dispatcher,
+						redirect: "manual",
+					})) as unknown as Response;
+
+					// Handle redirects manually to prevent SSRF via 301/302 location headers
+					if (
+						response.status >= 300 &&
+						response.status < 400 &&
+						response.headers.has("location")
+					) {
+						redirects++;
+						if (redirects > MAX_REDIRECTS) {
+							try {
+								await response.body?.cancel();
+							} catch (err) {
+								console.warn("WebFetcher cancel error:", err);
+							}
+							this.cache.delete(normalizedUrl);
+
+							return `Error: Too many redirects for ${targetUrl}`;
+						}
+
+						const location = response.headers.get("location") as string;
+						const nextUrl = new url.URL(location, currentUrl).toString();
+
+						safeIp = await this.isValidUrl(nextUrl);
+						if (!safeIp) {
+							try {
+								await response.body?.cancel();
+							} catch (err) {
+								console.warn("WebFetcher cancel error:", err);
+							}
+							this.cache.delete(normalizedUrl);
+
+							return `Error: Redirected to invalid or insecure URL (${nextUrl})`;
+						}
+
+						try {
+							await response.body?.cancel();
+						} catch (err) {
+							console.warn("WebFetcher cancel error:", err);
+						}
+						currentUrl = nextUrl;
+					} else {
+						break;
+					}
+				}
+
+				if (!response || !response.ok) {
+					const statusText = response?.statusText || "unknown error";
+					console.error(
+						`[WebFetcher] HTTP Error ${response?.status || "unknown"} - ${statusText} while fetching ${targetUrl}`,
+					);
+					try {
+						await response?.body?.cancel();
+					} catch (err) {
+						console.warn("WebFetcher cancel error:", err);
+					}
+					this.cache.delete(normalizedUrl);
+
+					return `Error: HTTP ${response?.status || "unknown"} from ${targetUrl}`;
+				}
+
+				const contentType = (
+					response.headers.get("content-type") || ""
+				).toLowerCase();
+				// Enforce strict allowlist of text-based content types to prevent downloading arbitrary large binaries
+				if (
+					contentType &&
+					!contentType.includes("text/") &&
+					!contentType.includes("application/json") &&
+					!contentType.includes("application/xml") &&
+					!contentType.includes("application/xhtml")
+				) {
+					try {
+						await response.body?.cancel();
+					} catch (err) {
+						console.warn("WebFetcher cancel error:", err);
+					}
+					this.cache.delete(normalizedUrl);
+
+					return `Error: Unsupported content type (${contentType}) from ${targetUrl}`;
+				}
+
+				let text = "";
+				if (response.body) {
+					reader = response.body.getReader();
+					let decoder: TextDecoder;
+					try {
+						decoder = new TextDecoder(extractCharset(contentType));
+					} catch {
+						decoder = new TextDecoder("utf-8");
+					}
+					let totalBytes = 0;
+					const MAX_BYTES = 500_000; // Limit payload size to avoid OOM
+					const chunks: string[] = [];
+					while (true) {
+						const { done, value } = await reader.read();
+						if (done) break;
+
+						totalBytes += value.length;
+						chunks.push(decoder.decode(value, { stream: true }));
+
+						if (totalBytes >= MAX_BYTES) {
+							// Cancel the reader early to save bandwidth and memory
+							try {
+								await reader.cancel();
+							} catch (err) {
+								console.warn("WebFetcher cancel error:", err);
+							}
+							break;
+						}
+					}
+					chunks.push(decoder.decode());
+					text = chunks.join("");
+				} else {
+					text = await response.text();
+				}
+
+				// Basic HTML to Text stripping (a real app would use cheerio or html-to-text)
+				// Note: Regex-based HTML stripping is limited and can struggle with severely malformed HTML.
+				// However, for the scope of extracting textual context for LLMs, these non-greedy regexes
+				// combined with the 500KB payload limit provide excellent performance and sufficient reliability
+				// without introducing large DOM parsing dependencies (which would impact startup time).
+				const strippedText = text
+					.replace(/<!--[\s\S]*?-->/g, "") // Remove HTML comments early. Note: Safe because <script>/<style> blocks are removed entirely in the next step.
+					.replace(
+						/<(script|style|svg|nav|footer|iframe|noscript)\b[^>]*\/>/gi,
+						"", // Remove self-closing boilerplate tags first
+					)
+					.replace(
+						/<(script|style|svg|nav|footer|iframe|noscript)\b[^>]*>[\s\S]*?(?:<\/\1\s*>|$)/gi,
+						"", // Remove complete and unclosed boilerplate blocks
+					)
+					.replace(/<[^>]+>|<[^>]*$/g, " ") // Remove complete HTML tags and any trailing partial HTML tag
+					.replace(/\s+/g, " ")
+					.trim();
+				const truncated = strippedText.slice(0, 8000); // Prevent context window explosion
+				return truncated;
+			} catch (error: unknown) {
+				console.error(
+					`[WebFetcher] Fetch failed for ${targetUrl}: ${error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error)}`,
+				);
+				if (reader) {
+					try {
+						await reader.cancel();
+					} catch (err) {
+						console.warn("WebFetcher cancel error:", err);
+					}
+				} else {
+					try {
+						await response?.body?.cancel();
+					} catch (err) {
+						console.warn("WebFetcher cancel error:", err);
+					}
+				}
+				this.cache.delete(normalizedUrl);
+
+				return `Error: Failed to fetch ${targetUrl} - ${error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error)}`;
+			} finally {
+				for (const d of createdDispatchers) {
+					try {
+						await d.close();
+					} catch (err) {}
+				}
+			}
+		})();
+
+		this.cache.set(normalizedUrl, fetchPromise);
+
+		return fetchPromise;
+	}
+
+	/**
+	 * Fetches multiple URLs with bounded concurrency.
+	 */
+	public async fetchBatch(urls: string[]): Promise<Map<string, string>> {
+		const results = new Map<string, string>();
+
+		// Pre-initialize to maintain exact input ranking order
+		for (const u of urls) {
+			results.set(u, "");
+		}
+
+		// Map normalized URL (no hash) to a set of original URLs that requested it
+		const normalizedToOriginals = new Map<string, Set<string>>();
+		for (const u of urls) {
+			try {
+				const parsed = new URL(u);
+				parsed.hash = "";
+				const normalized = parsed.toString();
+				if (!normalizedToOriginals.has(normalized)) {
+					normalizedToOriginals.set(normalized, new Set());
+				}
+				normalizedToOriginals.get(normalized)?.add(u);
+			} catch {
+				// If URL is completely invalid, treat it as its own unique target so it hits the error path
+				if (!normalizedToOriginals.has(u)) {
+					normalizedToOriginals.set(u, new Set());
+				}
+				normalizedToOriginals.get(u)?.add(u);
+			}
+		}
+
+		const limit = pLimit(this.maxConcurrency);
+		const tasks = Array.from(normalizedToOriginals.entries()).map(
+			([normalizedUrl, originalUrls]) => {
+				return limit(async () => {
+					const content = await this.fetchSingle(normalizedUrl);
+					for (const orig of originalUrls) {
+						results.set(orig, content);
+					}
+				});
+			},
+		);
+
+		await Promise.all(tasks);
+		return results;
+	}
+}

diff --git a/src/utils/config.ts b/src/utils/config.ts
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..72f8372
--- /dev/null
+++ b/src/utils/config.ts
@@ -0,0 +1,71 @@
+import * as fs from "node:fs/promises";
+import * as os from "node:os";
+import * as path from "node:path";
+
+export interface AutoResearchConfig {
+	OPENAI_API_KEY?: string;
+	OPENAI_MODEL?: string;
+	OPENAI_BASE_URL?: string;
+	SERPER_API_KEY?: string;
+	DEFAULT_OUTPUT_DIR?: string;
+	DEFAULT_FORMAT?: "markdown" | "html" | "json" | "all";
+}
+
+export class ConfigManager {
+	private readonly configDir: string;
+	private readonly configPath: string;
+	private configPromise: Promise<AutoResearchConfig> | null = null;
+
+	constructor() {
+		this.configDir = path.join(os.homedir(), ".autoresearch");
+		this.configPath = path.join(this.configDir, "config.json");
+	}
+
+	/**
+	 * Retrieves the current configuration.
+	 */
+	getConfig(): Promise<AutoResearchConfig> {
+		if (this.configPromise) {
+			return this.configPromise;
+		}
+
+		this.configPromise = (async () => {
+			try {
+				const data = await fs.readFile(this.configPath, "utf-8");
+				return JSON.parse(data);
+			} catch {
+				return {};
+			}
+		})();
+
+		return this.configPromise;
+	}
+
+	/**
+	 * Saves configuration securely with restricted file permissions (600).
+	 */
+	async setConfig(newConfig: Partial<AutoResearchConfig>): Promise<void> {
+		const current = await this.getConfig();
+		const merged = { ...current, ...newConfig };
+
+		await fs.mkdir(this.configDir, { recursive: true });
+
+		await fs.writeFile(this.configPath, JSON.stringify(merged, null, 2), {
+			encoding: "utf-8",
+			mode: 0o600, // Secure permissions: read/write for owner only
+		});
+
+		this.configPromise = Promise.resolve(merged);
+	}
+
+	/**
+	 * Gets a specific API key, falling back to process.env if available (for CI/CD).
+	 */
+	async get(key: keyof AutoResearchConfig): Promise<string | undefined> {
+		if (process.env[key]) {
+			return process.env[key];
+		}
+		const config = await this.getConfig();
+		return config[key];
+	}
+}
diff --git a/src/utils/http.ts b/src/utils/http.ts
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..8193bcd
--- /dev/null
+++ b/src/utils/http.ts
@@ -0,0 +1,13 @@
+/**
+ * Extracts the charset from a Content-Type HTTP header.
+ * Falls back to "utf-8" if no charset is found or the header is empty.
+ *
+ * @param contentType The raw Content-Type header string
+ * @returns The extracted charset string
+ */
+export function extractCharset(contentType: string): string {
+	if (!contentType) return "utf-8";
+
+	const charsetMatch = contentType.match(/charset\s*=\s*['"]?([\w-]+)['"]?/i);
+	return charsetMatch ? charsetMatch[1].trim().toLowerCase() : "utf-8";
+}

+import { beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
+import { DuckDuckGoSearcher } from "../src/tools/DuckDuckGoSearcher";
+
+// Mock global fetch
+global.fetch = vi.fn() as any;
+
+describe("DuckDuckGoSearcher", () => {
+	let searcher: DuckDuckGoSearcher;
+
+	beforeEach(() => {
+		searcher = new DuckDuckGoSearcher();
+		vi.clearAllMocks();
+	});
+
+	it("should return empty array for empty or whitespace query", async () => {
+		const res1 = await searcher.search("");
+		const res2 = await searcher.search("   ");
+		expect(res1).toEqual([]);
+		expect(res2).toEqual([]);
+		expect(global.fetch).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
+	});
+
+	it("should parse HTML results, unwrap uddg links, and decode HTML entities", async () => {
+		const sampleHtml = `
+		<html>
+		<body>
+			<div class="result results_links">
+				<a class="result__a" href="//duckduckgo.com/l/?uddg=https%3A%2F%2Fexample.com%2Fpost%3Fid%3D123%26topic%3Dai&rut=1"><b>Example</b> &amp; Insights</a>
+				<a class="result__snippet">This is an &quot;innovative&quot; article &#39;about&#39; LLMs &amp; AI agents.</a>
+			</div>
+			<div class="result results_links">
+				<a class="result__a" href="https://direct-link.org">Direct &lt;Link&gt;</a>
+				<a class="result__snippet">Detailed exploration of multi-vector search.</a>
+			</div>
+		</body>
+		</html>
+		`;
+
+		(global.fetch as any).mockResolvedValueOnce({
+			ok: true,
+			text: async () => sampleHtml,
+		});
+
+		const results = await searcher.search("test query", 5);
+		expect(results.length).toBe(2);
+
+		expect(results[0].title).toBe("Example & Insights");
+		expect(results[0].link).toBe("https://example.com/post?id=123&topic=ai");
+		expect(results[0].snippet).toBe(
+			'This is an "innovative" article \'about\' LLMs & AI agents.',
+		);
+
+		expect(results[1].title).toBe("Direct <Link>");
+		expect(results[1].link).toBe("https://direct-link.org/");
+		expect(results[1].snippet).toBe(
+			"Detailed exploration of multi-vector search.",
+		);
+	});
+
+	it("should cache identical search requests in memory", async () => {
+		const sampleHtml = `
+		<div class="result">
+			<a class="result__a" href="https://cached.org">Cached Title</a>
+			<a class="result__snippet">Cached Snippet</a>
+		</div>
+		`;
+
+		(global.fetch as any).mockResolvedValueOnce({
+			ok: true,
+			text: async () => sampleHtml,
+		});
+
+		const firstCall = await searcher.search("cached query", 2);
+		const secondCall = await searcher.search("cached query", 2);
+
+		expect(firstCall).toEqual(secondCall);
+		expect(global.fetch).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
+	});
+
+	it("should handle empty or malformed HTML safely without throwing", async () => {
+		(global.fetch as any).mockResolvedValueOnce({
+			ok: true,
+			text: async () => "<html><body>No matching results</body></html>",
+		});
+
+		const results = await searcher.search("obscure impossible query", 5);
+		expect(results).toEqual([]);
+	});
+
+	it("should catch fetch errors and return empty array gracefully", async () => {
+		(global.fetch as any).mockRejectedValue(new Error("Network failure"));
+
+		const results = await searcher.search("error query", 2);
+		expect(results).toEqual([]);
+	});
+});

+import { describe, it, expect, beforeEach, vi } from "vitest";
+import { GoogleSearcher } from "../src/tools/GoogleSearcher";
+import { ConfigManager } from "../src/utils/config";
+
+// Mock ConfigManager mapping
+vi.mock("../src/utils/config", () => {
+	return {
+		ConfigManager: class {
+			get = vi.fn().mockResolvedValue("test-serper-key");
+		},
+	};
+});
+
+// Mock global fetch
+global.fetch = vi.fn() as any;
+
+describe("GoogleSearcher", () => {
+	let searcher: GoogleSearcher;
+
+	beforeEach(() => {
+		searcher = new GoogleSearcher();
+		vi.clearAllMocks();
+	});
+
+	it("should use injected configManager", async () => {
+		const customConfigManager = new ConfigManager();
+		customConfigManager.get = vi.fn().mockResolvedValue("custom-key");
+		const customSearcher = new GoogleSearcher(customConfigManager);
+		(global.fetch as any).mockResolvedValueOnce({
+			ok: true,
+			json: async () => ({ organic: [] }),
+		});
+		await customSearcher.search("test");
+		expect(customConfigManager.get).toHaveBeenCalledWith("SERPER_API_KEY");
+	});
+
+	it("should return search results on successful API call", async () => {
+		const mockResponse = {
+			organic: [
+				{ title: "Result 1", link: "https://r1.com", snippet: "Snippet 1" },
+				{ title: "Result 2", link: "https://r2.com", snippet: "Snippet 2" },
+			],
+		};
+
+		(global.fetch as any).mockResolvedValueOnce({
+			ok: true,
+			json: async () => mockResponse,
+		});
+
+		const results = await searcher.search("test query", 2);
+
+		expect(results).toHaveLength(2);
+		expect(results[0].title).toBe("Result 1");
+	});
+
+	it("should retry with exponential backoff on failure", async () => {
+		// Mock setTimeout to fire immediately for tests
+		vi.spyOn(global, "setTimeout").mockImplementation((cb: any) => {
+			cb();
+			return 0 as any;
+		});
+
+		// Fail twice, succeed on the third time
+		(global.fetch as any)
+			.mockRejectedValueOnce(new Error("Network Error 1"))
+			.mockRejectedValueOnce(new Error("Network Error 2"))
+			.mockResolvedValueOnce({
+				ok: true,
+				json: async () => ({
+					organic: [{ title: "Success", link: "s", snippet: "s" }],
+				}),
+			});
+
+		const results = await searcher.search("retry test", 1);
+
+		expect(results).toHaveLength(1);
+		expect(results[0].title).toBe("Success");
+		expect(global.fetch).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(3);
+
+		vi.restoreAllMocks();
+	});
+
+	it("should throw error if max retries exceeded", async () => {
+		vi.spyOn(global, "setTimeout").mockImplementation((cb: any) => {
+			cb();
+			return 0 as any;
+		});
+
+		// Always fail
+		(global.fetch as any).mockRejectedValue(
+			new Error("Persistent Network Error"),
+		);
+
+		await expect(searcher.search("fail test", 1)).rejects.toThrow(
+			"Persistent Network Error",
+		);
+
+		// Should have tried 3 times (the maxRetries default)
+		expect(global.fetch).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(3);
+
+		vi.restoreAllMocks();
+	});
+
+	it("should fail fast without retrying on 4xx client errors (except 429)", async () => {
+		vi.spyOn(global, "setTimeout").mockImplementation((cb: any) => {
+			cb();
+			return 0 as any;
+		});
+
+		(global.fetch as any).mockResolvedValue({
+			ok: false,
+			status: 403,
+			body: { cancel: vi.fn().mockResolvedValue(undefined) },
+		});
+
+		await expect(searcher.search("fail fast test", 1)).rejects.toThrow(
+			"Serper API HTTP error: 403",
+		);
+
+		// Should only try once because it fails fast
+		expect(global.fetch).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
+
+		vi.restoreAllMocks();
+	});
+
+	it("should return empty array immediately for empty or whitespace query without network call", async () => {
+		const resultEmpty = await searcher.search("");
+		const resultWhitespace = await searcher.search("   ");
+
+		expect(resultEmpty).toEqual([]);
+		expect(resultWhitespace).toEqual([]);
+		expect(global.fetch).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
+	});
+
+	it("should filter out results missing valid links and safely handle null or undefined titles and snippets", async () => {
+		const mockResponse = {
+			organic: [
+				{ title: "Valid Title", link: "https://valid.com", snippet: "Valid snippet" },
+				{ title: null, link: "https://notitle.com", snippet: undefined },
+				{ title: "No Link", link: "", snippet: "Should be filtered" },
+				{ title: "Missing Link", snippet: "Should be filtered" },
+				null,
+			],
+		};
+
+		(global.fetch as any).mockResolvedValueOnce({
+			ok: true,
+			json: async () => mockResponse,
+		});
+
+		const results = await searcher.search("sanitize test", 5);
+
+		expect(results).toHaveLength(2);
+		expect(results[0]).toEqual({
+			title: "Valid Title",
+			link: "https://valid.com",
+			snippet: "Valid snippet",
+		});
+		expect(results[1]).toEqual({
+			title: "",
+			link: "https://notitle.com",
+			snippet: "",
+		});
+	});
+});

+import { beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
+import { SearchManager } from "../src/tools/SearchManager";
+import { ConfigManager } from "../src/utils/config";
+
+// Mock DuckDuckGoSearcher
+vi.mock("../src/tools/DuckDuckGoSearcher", () => {
+	return {
+		DuckDuckGoSearcher: class {
+			search = vi.fn().mockResolvedValue([
+				{
+					title: "DDG Result",
+					link: "https://duckduckgo-source.com/page",
+					snippet: "DDG snippet content",
+				},
+			]);
+		},
+	};
+});
+
+// Mock GoogleSearcher
+vi.mock("../src/tools/GoogleSearcher", () => {
+	return {
+		GoogleSearcher: class {
+			search = vi.fn().mockResolvedValue([
+				{
+					title: "Serper Result",
+					link: "https://google-source.com/article",
+					snippet: "Serper snippet content",
+				},
+			]);
+		},
+	};
+});
+
+describe("SearchManager", () => {
+	let configManager: ConfigManager;
+	let manager: SearchManager;
+
+	beforeEach(() => {
+		configManager = new ConfigManager();
+		manager = new SearchManager(configManager);
+		vi.clearAllMocks();
+	});
+
+	it("should prioritize GoogleSearcher when SERPER_API_KEY is configured", async () => {
+		configManager.get = vi.fn().mockImplementation(async (key: string) => {
+			if (key === "SERPER_API_KEY") return "valid-serper-key";
+			return undefined;
+		});
+
+		const results = await manager.search("AI agents", 5);
+		expect(results.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
+		expect(results[0].title).toBe("Serper Result");
+		expect(results[0].link).toBe("https://google-source.com/article");
+	});
+
+	it("should fallback to DuckDuckGo when SERPER_API_KEY is missing", async () => {
+		configManager.get = vi.fn().mockResolvedValue(undefined);
+
+		const results = await manager.search("Zero config query", 5);
+		expect(results.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
+		expect(results[0].title).toBe("DDG Result");
+		expect(results[0].link).toBe("https://duckduckgo-source.com/page");
+	});
+
+	it("should fallback to DuckDuckGo when GoogleSearcher throws an error", async () => {
+		configManager.get = vi.fn().mockImplementation(async (key: string) => {
+			if (key === "SERPER_API_KEY") return "valid-serper-key";
+			return undefined;
+		});
+
+		// Force googleSearcher to reject
+		(manager as any).googleSearcher.search.mockRejectedValueOnce(
+			new Error("Rate limit 429"),
+		);
+
+		const results = await manager.search("Fallback on error", 3);
+		expect(results.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
+		expect(results[0].title).toBe("DDG Result");
+	});
+
+	it("should return empty array when query is empty", async () => {
+		const results = await manager.search("   ");
+		expect(results).toEqual([]);
+	});
+
+	it("should execute searchMulti and deduplicate results by normalized URL", async () => {
+		configManager.get = vi.fn().mockResolvedValue(undefined);
+
+		(manager as any).ddgSearcher.search = vi
+			.fn()
+			.mockImplementation(async (q: string) => {
+				if (q.includes("architecture")) {
+					return [
+						{
+							title: "Architecture Guide",
+							link: "https://example.com/guide#part-1",
+							snippet: "Part 1 overview",
+						},
+						{
+							title: "Shared Article",
+							link: "https://shared.org/research",
+							snippet: "Shared overview",
+						},
+					];
+				}
+				return [
+					{
+						title: "Benchmarks",
+						link: "https://example.com/benchmarks",
+						snippet: "Performance stats",
+					},
+					{
+						title: "Shared Article Dup",
+						link: "https://shared.org/research#section2", // same normalized URL
+						snippet: "Duplicate source snippet",
+					},
+				];
+			});
+
+		const results = await manager.searchMulti(
+			["AI architecture", "AI benchmarks", "AI architecture"], // duplicate query handled
+			3,
+		);
+
+		expect(results.length).toBe(3);
+		const links = results.map((r) => r.link);
+		expect(links).toContain("https://example.com/guide#part-1");
+		expect(links).toContain("https://shared.org/research");
+		expect(links).toContain("https://example.com/benchmarks");
+	});
+
+	it("should return empty array for searchMulti when given empty query array", async () => {
+		const results = await manager.searchMulti(["  ", ""]);
+		expect(results).toEqual([]);
+	});
+});

+import { beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
+import * as undici from "undici";
+
+vi.mock("undici", () => {
+	return {
+		Agent: class {},
+		fetch: vi.fn(),
+	};
+});
+
+import { WebFetcher } from "../src/tools/WebFetcher";
+
+describe("WebFetcher", () => {
+	let fetcher: WebFetcher;
+
+	beforeEach(() => {
+		fetcher = new WebFetcher(3);
+		// Clear cache
+		(fetcher as any).cache.clear();
+	});
+
+	it("should preserve semantic HTML but strip boilerplate like <nav> and <footer>", async () => {
+
+		vi.mocked(undici.fetch).mockImplementation(async () => {
+			return {
+				status: 200,
+				headers: new Headers({ "content-type": "text/html" }),
+				ok: true,
+				text: async () => `
+					<html>
+						<body>
+							<nav>Should be stripped</nav>
+							<header><h1>Semantic Title</h1></header>
+							<main>Main content here</main>
+							<footer>Should also be stripped</footer>
+							<iframe src="ads"></iframe>
+							<noscript>No JS</noscript>
+						</body>
+					</html>
+				`,
+			};
+		});
+
+		const result = await (fetcher as any).fetchSingle("https://example.com/test-html");
+
+		expect(result).toContain("Semantic Title");
+		expect(result).toContain("Main content here");
+
+		expect(result).not.toContain("Should be stripped");
+		expect(result).not.toContain("Should also be stripped");
+		expect(result).not.toContain("ads");
+		expect(result).not.toContain("No JS");
+
+		vi.mocked(undici.fetch).mockReset();
+	});
+
+	it("should properly strip nested boilerplate tags", async () => {
+
+		vi.mocked(undici.fetch).mockImplementation(async () => {
+			return {
+				status: 200,
+				headers: new Headers({ "content-type": "text/html" }),
+				ok: true,
+				text: async () => `
+					<html>
+						<body>
+							<nav>
+								<ul>
+									<li><a href="#">Link 1</a></li>
+									<li><script>console.log("nested script")</script></li>
+								</ul>
+								<div>Some text</div>
+								<iframe src="inner" />
+							</nav>
+							<main>Main content</main>
+						</body>
+					</html>
+				`,
+			};
+		});
+
+		const result = await (fetcher as any).fetchSingle("https://example.com/test-nested");
+
+		expect(result).toContain("Main content");
+
+		expect(result).not.toContain("Link 1");
+		expect(result).not.toContain("nested script");
+		expect(result).not.toContain("Some text");
+		expect(result).not.toContain("inner");
+
+		vi.mocked(undici.fetch).mockReset();
+	});
+
+	it("should handle edge cases with self-closing boilerplate tags and tags with unusual attributes", async () => {
+
+		vi.mocked(undici.fetch).mockImplementation(async () => {
+			return {
+				status: 200,
+				headers: new Headers({ "content-type": "text/html" }),
+				ok: true,
+				text: async () => `
+					<html>
+						<body>
+							<nav class="super-weird" data-custom="yes" >Strip this</nav>
+							<iframe src="tracker" width="0" height="0" />
+							<noscript aria-hidden="true" >No JS</noscript>
+							<footer
+								id="main-footer"
+							>
+								Multi-line attributes
+							</footer>
+							<main>Keep this content</main>
+						</body>
+					</html>
+				`,
+			};
+		});
+
+		const result = await (fetcher as any).fetchSingle("https://example.com/test-edge-cases");
+
+		expect(result).toContain("Keep this content");
+
+		expect(result).not.toContain("Strip this");
+		expect(result).not.toContain("Multi-line attributes");
+		expect(result).not.toContain("No JS");
+		expect(result).not.toContain("tracker");
+
+		vi.mocked(undici.fetch).mockReset();
+	});
+
+	it("should reject domains that resolve to empty address arrays", async () => {
+		const dnsPromises = require("node:dns/promises");
+		const originalLookup = dnsPromises.lookup;
+		dnsPromises.lookup = vi.fn().mockResolvedValue([]);
+
+		const result = await (fetcher as any).fetchSingle(
+			"http://empty-dns.example.com",
+		);
+		expect(result).toContain("Error: Invalid or insecure URL");
+
+		dnsPromises.lookup = originalLookup;
+	});
+
+	it("should reject SSRF URLs", async () => {
+		const resultLocal = await (fetcher as any).fetchSingle(
+			"http://localhost:8080/admin",
+		);
+		expect(resultLocal).toContain("Error: Invalid or insecure URL");
+
+		const resultInternal = await (fetcher as any).fetchSingle(
+			"http://192.168.1.5/keys",
+		);
+		expect(resultInternal).toContain("Error: Invalid or insecure URL");
+
+		const resultFile = await (fetcher as any).fetchSingle("file:///etc/passwd");
+		expect(resultFile).toContain("Error: Invalid or insecure URL");
+
+		const resultAwsMeta = await (fetcher as any).fetchSingle(
+			"http://169.254.169.254/latest/meta-data/",
+		);
+		expect(resultAwsMeta).toContain("Error: Invalid or insecure URL");
+
+		const resultZero = await (fetcher as any).fetchSingle(
+			"http://0.0.0.0:8000/api",
+		);
+		expect(resultZero).toContain("Error: Invalid or insecure URL");
+
+		const resultLoopback = await (fetcher as any).fetchSingle(
+			"http://127.0.1.1/admin",
+		);
+		expect(resultLoopback).toContain("Error: Invalid or insecure URL");
+
+		const resultIpv6 = await (fetcher as any).fetchSingle(
+			"http://[::1]:8080/admin",
+		);
+		expect(resultIpv6).toContain("Error: Invalid or insecure URL");
+
+		const resultIpv6Unspecified = await (fetcher as any).fetchSingle(
+			"http://[::]:8080/admin",
+		);
+		expect(resultIpv6Unspecified).toContain("Error: Invalid or insecure URL");
+
+		const resultIpv4Mapped = await (fetcher as any).fetchSingle(
+			"http://[::ffff:127.0.0.1]:8080/admin",
+		);
+		expect(resultIpv4Mapped).toContain("Error: Invalid or insecure URL");
+	});
+
+	it("should reject redirects to SSRF URLs", async () => {
+		// Mock undici.fetch to return a 302 redirect to an internal IP
+
+		vi.mocked(undici.fetch).mockImplementation(async (url, options) => {
+			if (url === "https://example.com/redirect-to-internal") {
+				return {
+					status: 302,
+					headers: new Headers({
+						location: "http://169.254.169.254/latest/meta-data/",
+					}),
+					ok: false,
+				};
+			}
+			return {
+				status: 200,
+				headers: new Headers({ "content-type": "text/html" }),
+				ok: true,
+				text: async () => "Mock content",
+			};
+		});
+
+		const result = await (fetcher as any).fetchSingle(
+			"https://example.com/redirect-to-internal",
+		);
+		expect(result).toContain("Error: Redirected to invalid or insecure URL");
+
+		vi.mocked(undici.fetch).mockReset();
+	});
+
+	it("should correctly handle and strip malformed nested boilerplate tags", async () => {
+
+
+		vi.mocked(undici.fetch).mockImplementation(async () => {
+			return {
+				status: 200,
+				headers: new Headers({ "content-type": "text/html" }),
+				ok: true,
+				text: async () => `
+					<html>
+						<body>
+							<p>Important Content</p>
+							<footer>
+								<nav>
+									<!-- Malformed unclosed tags inside -->
+									<ul><li><noscript>Malformed <iframe> Content
+								</nav>
+							</footer>
+						</body>
+					</html>

+			};
+		});
+
+		const result = await (fetcher as any).fetchSingle("https://example.com/malformed-nested-strip");

+		expect(result).toContain("Important Content");
+		expect(result).not.toContain("Malformed");
+		expect(result).not.toContain("Malformed <iframe> Content");

+	});
+
+	it("should cancel unconsumed response bodies during redirect loops to prevent socket leaks", async () => {
+
+		const mockCancel = vi.fn().mockResolvedValue(undefined);
+
+		vi.mocked(undici.fetch).mockImplementation(async (url) => {
+			// Force an invalid redirect loop to trigger the body.cancel() behavior
+			return {
+				status: 302,
+				headers: new Headers({
+					location: "http://169.254.169.254/latest/meta-data/",
+				}),
+				ok: false,
+				body: { cancel: mockCancel },
+			};
+		});
+
+		const result = await (fetcher as any).fetchSingle(
+			"https://example.com/loop",
+		);
+		expect(result).toContain("Error: Redirected to invalid or insecure URL");
+		expect(mockCancel).toHaveBeenCalled();
+
+		vi.mocked(undici.fetch).mockReset();
+	});
+
+	it("should reject non-text content types", async () => {
+
+		const mockCancel = vi.fn().mockResolvedValue(undefined);
+
+		vi.mocked(undici.fetch).mockImplementation(async (url) => {
+			return {
+				ok: true,
+				status: 200,
+				headers: new Headers({
+					"content-type": "application/zip",
+				}),
+				body: { cancel: mockCancel },
+			};
+		});
+
+		const result = await (fetcher as any).fetchSingle(
+			"https://example.com/archive.zip",
+		);
+		expect(result).toContain(
+			"Error: Unsupported content type (application/zip)",
+		);
+		expect(mockCancel).toHaveBeenCalled();
+
+		vi.mocked(undici.fetch).mockReset();
+	});

+	it("should allow valid public HTTP/HTTPS URLs including tricky ones", async () => {
+		const isValid = await (fetcher as any).isValidUrl(
+			"https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/AI",
+		);
+		expect(typeof isValid).toBe("string");
+
+		// To test real DNS we need a domain that actually resolves to a public IP.
+		// 10.example.com may not resolve, so we'll mock dns.lookup or pick known public domains.
+		// We will just test with real public domains for this assertion since dns is not mocked.
+		const isValidTen = await (fetcher as any).isValidUrl(
+			"http://example.com/foo",
+		);
+		expect(typeof isValidTen).toBe("string");
+
+		const isValidInternalLooking = await (fetcher as any).isValidUrl(
+			"https://example.org",
+		);
+		expect(typeof isValidInternalLooking).toBe("string");
+	});
+
+	it("should cache results to avoid redundant fetching", async () => {
+		const targetUrl = "https://example.com/unique-test";
+
+		// Pre-populate cache directly using normalized URL
+		(fetcher as any).cache.set(
+			"https://example.com/unique-test",
+			"Cached Data Content",
+		);

+		expect(result).toBe("Cached Data Content");
+	});

+	it("should deduplicate batch fetches with different hash fragments", async () => {
+
+		const fetchMock = vi.fn().mockImplementation(async () => {
+			return {
+				status: 200,
+				headers: new Headers({ "content-type": "text/html" }),
+				ok: true,
+				text: async () => "Mocked content",
+			};
+		});
+		vi.mocked(undici.fetch).mockImplementation(fetchMock);
+
+		// Since dns.lookup cannot be easily redefined, we can just spy on fetchSingle instead,
+		// but since it's private we can just use the undici.fetch mock.
+		// Real dns.lookup for example.com works and returns a public IP, so it passes SSRF checks.
+
+		const urls = [
+			"https://example.com/docs#section1",
+			"https://example.com/docs#section2",
+			"https://example.com/docs",
+		];
+
+		const results = await fetcher.fetchBatch(urls);
+
+		// Global fetch should only be called once because all three URLs map to the same resource
+		expect(fetchMock).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
+
+		expect(results.get("https://example.com/docs#section1")).toBe(
+			"Mocked content",
+		);
+		expect(results.get("https://example.com/docs#section2")).toBe(
+			"Mocked content",
+		);
+		expect(results.get("https://example.com/docs")).toBe("Mocked content");
+
+		vi.mocked(undici.fetch).mockReset();
+	});
+
+	it("should decode response body correctly using charset from Content-Type", async () => {
+		const fetcher = new WebFetcher(3);
+
+		vi.mocked(undici.fetch).mockImplementation(async () => {
+			return {
+				status: 200,
+				headers: new Headers({ "content-type": "text/html; charset=ISO-8859-1" }),
+				ok: true,
+				body: {
+					getReader: () => {
+						let done = false;
+						return {
+							read: async () => {
+								if (!done) {
+									done = true;
+									// 0xe9 is 'é' in ISO-8859-1
+									return { done: false, value: new Uint8Array([0xe9]) };
+								}
+								return { done: true, value: undefined };
+							},
+							cancel: async () => {},
+						};
+					},
+				},
+			};
+		});
+
+		const result = await (fetcher as any).fetchSingle("https://example.com/iso-test");
+		expect(result).toBe("é");
+
+		vi.mocked(undici.fetch).mockReset();
+	});
+
+	it("should fallback to utf-8 if charset is unsupported", async () => {
+		const fetcher = new WebFetcher(3);
+
+		vi.mocked(undici.fetch).mockImplementation(async () => {
+			return {
+				status: 200,
+				headers: new Headers({ "content-type": "text/html; charset=unsupported-charset" }),
+				ok: true,
+				body: {
+					getReader: () => {
+						let done = false;
+						return {
+							read: async () => {
+								if (!done) {
+									done = true;
+									return { done: false, value: new Uint8Array([0x61]) }; // 'a'
+								}
+								return { done: true, value: undefined };
+							},
+							cancel: async () => {},
+						};
+					},
+				},
+			};
+		});
+
+		const result = await (fetcher as any).fetchSingle("https://example.com/fallback-test");
+		expect(result).toBe("a");
+
+		vi.mocked(undici.fetch).mockReset();
+	});
+
+	it("should handle extra spaces in Content-Type gracefully", async () => {
+		const fetcher = new WebFetcher(3);
+
+
+		vi.mocked(undici.fetch).mockImplementation(async () => {
+			const encoder = new TextEncoder();
+			const encoded = encoder.encode("Spaced Test Content");
+			const stream = new ReadableStream({
+				start(controller) {
+					controller.enqueue(encoded);
+					controller.close();
+				}
+			});
+			return {
+				status: 200,
+				headers: new Headers({ "content-type": "text/html; charset  =  windows-1252" }),
+				ok: true,
+				body: stream,
+			};
+		});
+		const resultSpaced = await (fetcher as any).fetchSingle("https://example.com/spaced-charset");
+		expect(resultSpaced).toBe("Spaced Test Content");
+
+		vi.mocked(undici.fetch).mockReset();
+	});
+
+	it("should handle missing charset in Content-Type gracefully", async () => {
+		const fetcher = new WebFetcher(3);
+
+
+		vi.mocked(undici.fetch).mockImplementation(async () => {
+			const encoder = new TextEncoder();
+			const encoded = encoder.encode("Missing Test Content");
+			const stream = new ReadableStream({
+				start(controller) {
+					controller.enqueue(encoded);
+					controller.close();
+				}
+			});
+			return {
+				status: 200,
+				headers: new Headers({ "content-type": "text/html" }),
+				ok: true,
+				body: stream,
+			};
+		});
+		const resultMissing = await (fetcher as any).fetchSingle("https://example.com/missing-charset");
+		expect(resultMissing).toBe("Missing Test Content");
+
+		vi.mocked(undici.fetch).mockReset();
+	});
+
+	it("should gracefully handle malformed or non-UTF-8 charsets in Content-Type header", async () => {
+
+
+		// 1. Valid non-utf-8 charset
+		vi.mocked(undici.fetch).mockImplementation(async () => {
+			const encoder = new TextEncoder();
+			const encoded = encoder.encode("Valid Test Content");
+			const stream = new ReadableStream({
+				start(controller) {
+					controller.enqueue(encoded);
+					controller.close();
+				}
+			});
+			return {
+				status: 200,
+				headers: new Headers({ "content-type": "text/html; charset=windows-1252" }),
+				ok: true,
+				body: stream,
+			};
+		});
+		let result = await (fetcher as any).fetchSingle("https://example.com/windows-1252");
+		expect(result).toBe("Valid Test Content");
+		(fetcher as any).cache.clear();
+
+		// 2. Malformed/Unsupported charset fallback to utf-8
+		vi.mocked(undici.fetch).mockImplementation(async () => {
+			const encoder = new TextEncoder();
+			const encoded = encoder.encode("Malformed Test Content");
+			const stream = new ReadableStream({
+				start(controller) {
+					controller.enqueue(encoded);
+					controller.close();
+				}
+			});
+			return {
+				status: 200,
+				headers: new Headers({ "content-type": "text/html; charset=invalid-charset" }),
+				ok: true,
+				body: stream,
+			};
+		});
+		result = await (fetcher as any).fetchSingle("https://example.com/malformed-charset");
+		expect(result).toBe("Malformed Test Content");
+
+		// 3. Extra spaces in Content-Type
+		vi.mocked(undici.fetch).mockImplementation(async () => {
+			const encoder = new TextEncoder();
+			const encoded = encoder.encode("Spaced Test Content");
+			const stream = new ReadableStream({
+				start(controller) {
+					controller.enqueue(encoded);
+					controller.close();
+				}
+			});
+			return {
+				status: 200,
+				headers: new Headers({ "content-type": "text/html; charset  =  windows-1252" }),
+				ok: true,
+				body: stream,
+			};
+		});
+		const resultSpaced = await (fetcher as any).fetchSingle("https://example.com/spaced-charset");
+		expect(resultSpaced).toBe("Spaced Test Content");
+		(fetcher as any).cache.clear();
+
+		// 4. Missing charset
+		vi.mocked(undici.fetch).mockImplementation(async () => {
+			const encoder = new TextEncoder();
+			const encoded = encoder.encode("Missing Test Content");
+			const stream = new ReadableStream({
+				start(controller) {
+					controller.enqueue(encoded);
+					controller.close();
+				}
+			});
+			return {
+				status: 200,
+				headers: new Headers({ "content-type": "text/html" }),
+				ok: true,
+				body: stream,
+			};
+		});
+		const resultMissing = await (fetcher as any).fetchSingle("https://example.com/missing-charset");
+		expect(resultMissing).toBe("Missing Test Content");
+		vi.mocked(undici.fetch).mockReset();
+	});
+
+	it("should decode different charsets correctly and fallback to utf-8 safely", async () => {
+
+		vi.mocked(undici.fetch).mockImplementation(async (url) => {
+			if (url.includes("iso")) {
+				return {
+					status: 200,
+					headers: new Headers({ "content-type": "text/html; charset=iso-8859-1" }),
+					ok: true,
+					body: {
+						getReader: () => {
+							const text = "H\xe9llo W\xf6rld";
+							const uint8Array = new Uint8Array(text.length);
+							for (let i = 0; i < text.length; i++) {
+								uint8Array[i] = text.charCodeAt(i);
+							}
+							let done = false;
+							return {
+								read: async () => {
+									if (done) return { done: true, value: undefined };
+									done = true;
+									return { done: false, value: uint8Array };
+								},
+								cancel: vi.fn()
+							};
+						}
+					}
+				};
+			} else if (url.includes("unsupported")) {
+				return {
+					status: 200,
+					headers: new Headers({ "content-type": "text/html; charset=unsupported-charset" }),
+					ok: true,
+					body: {
+						getReader: () => {
+							const text = "Fallback to UTF-8";
+							const uint8Array = new Uint8Array(text.length);
+							for (let i = 0; i < text.length; i++) {
+								uint8Array[i] = text.charCodeAt(i);
+							}
+							let done = false;
+							return {
+								read: async () => {
+									if (done) return { done: true, value: undefined };
+									done = true;
+									return { done: false, value: uint8Array };
+								},
+								cancel: vi.fn()
+							};
+						}
+					}
+				};
+			} else {
+				return {
+					status: 200,
+					headers: new Headers({ "content-type": "text/html" }),
+					ok: true,
+					body: {
+						getReader: () => {
+							const text = "No charset provided";
+							const uint8Array = new Uint8Array(text.length);
+							for (let i = 0; i < text.length; i++) {
+								uint8Array[i] = text.charCodeAt(i);
+							}
+							let done = false;
+							return {
+								read: async () => {
+									if (done) return { done: true, value: undefined };
+									done = true;
+									return { done: false, value: uint8Array };
+								},
+								cancel: vi.fn()
+							};
+						}
+					}
+				};
+			}
+		});
+
+		const resultIso = await (fetcher as any).fetchSingle("https://example.com/iso");
+		expect(resultIso).toContain("Héllo Wörld");
+
+		const resultUnsupported = await (fetcher as any).fetchSingle("https://example.com/unsupported");
+		expect(resultUnsupported).toContain("Fallback to UTF-8");
+
+		const resultNoCharset = await (fetcher as any).fetchSingle("https://example.com/no-charset");
+		expect(resultNoCharset).toContain("No charset provided");
+
+		vi.mocked(undici.fetch).mockReset();
+	});
+
+	it("should evict normalizedUrl from cache on fetch error", async () => {
+
+		vi.mocked(undici.fetch).mockImplementation(async () => {
+			return {
+				status: 500,
+				headers: new Headers({ "content-type": "text/html" }),
+				ok: false,
+				body: { cancel: vi.fn().mockResolvedValue(undefined) },
+			};
+		});
+
+		const targetUrl = "https://example.com/error-test#section";
+		const normalizedUrl = "https://example.com/error-test";
+		await fetcher["fetchSingle"](targetUrl);
+
+		expect((fetcher as any).cache.has(normalizedUrl)).toBe(false);
+
+		vi.mocked(undici.fetch).mockReset();
+	});
+
+	it("should remove both targetUrl and normalizedUrl from cache on failure", async () => {
+
+		vi.mocked(undici.fetch).mockImplementation(async () => {
+			return {
+				status: 404,
+				headers: new Headers({ "content-type": "text/html" }),
+				ok: false,
+				body: { cancel: vi.fn().mockResolvedValue(undefined) },
+			};
+		});
+
+		const targetUrl = "https://example.com/not-found#section";
+		const normalizedUrl = "https://example.com/not-found";
+
+		const result = await (fetcher as any).fetchSingle(targetUrl);
+		expect(result).toContain(
+			"Error: HTTP 404 from https://example.com/not-found#section",
+		);
+
+		// Cache should not contain either URL
+		expect((fetcher as any).cache.has(targetUrl)).toBe(false);
+		expect((fetcher as any).cache.has(normalizedUrl)).toBe(false);
+
+		vi.mocked(undici.fetch).mockReset();
+	});
+
+	it("should preserve input URL order in the results map regardless of fetch completion time", async () => {
+
+
+		vi.mocked(undici.fetch).mockImplementation(async (url) => {
+			if (url.includes("slow")) {
+				await new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, 50));
+			}
+			return {
+				status: 200,
+				headers: new Headers({ "content-type": "text/html" }),
+				ok: true,
+				text: async () => `Mock ${url}`,
+			};
+		});
+
+		const urls = [
+			"https://example.com/slow",
+			"https://example.com/fast",
+		];
+
+		const results = await fetcher.fetchBatch(urls);
+		expect(Array.from(results.keys())).toEqual(urls);
+
+		vi.mocked(undici.fetch).mockReset();
+	});
+
+	it("should strip HTML comments safely", async () => {
+
+		vi.mocked(undici.fetch).mockImplementation(async () => {
+			return {
+				status: 200,
+				headers: new Headers({ "content-type": "text/html" }),
+				ok: true,
+				text: async () => "<!-- <div> hidden > text --> <p>Main content</p>"
+			};
+		});
+
+		const result = await (fetcher as any).fetchSingle("https://example.com/test-strip-comments");
+		expect(result).toBe("Main content");
+
+		vi.mocked(undici.fetch).mockReset();
+	});
+
+	it("should correctly handle HTML comments within script tags without corrupting them", async () => {
+		const fetcher = new WebFetcher(3);
+
+		vi.mocked(undici.fetch).mockImplementation(async () => {
+			return {
+				status: 200,
+				headers: new Headers({ "content-type": "text/html" }),
+				ok: true,
+				text: async () => "<script>\n<!--\nconsole.log('test');\n//-->\n</script><p>Main content</p>"
+			};
+		});
+
+		const result = await (fetcher as any).fetchSingle("https://example.com/test-script-comments");
+		expect(result).toBe("Main content");
+
+		vi.mocked(undici.fetch).mockReset();
+	});
+
+	it("should strip boilerplate HTML tags to save context", async () => {
+
+		vi.mocked(undici.fetch).mockImplementation(async () => {
+			return {
+				status: 200,
+				headers: new Headers({ "content-type": "text/html" }),
+				ok: true,
+				text: async () => "<nav>Navigation</nav><footer>Footer</footer><noscript>No JS</noscript><iframe>Ads</iframe><p>Main content</p>"
+			};
+		});
+
+		const result = await (fetcher as any).fetchSingle("https://example.com/test-strip");
+		expect(result).toBe("Main content");
+
+		vi.mocked(undici.fetch).mockReset();
+	});
+
+	it("should strip HTML comments to save context tokens", async () => {
+		const fetcher = new WebFetcher(3);
+
+		vi.mocked(undici.fetch).mockImplementation(async () => {
+			return {
+				status: 200,
+				headers: new Headers({ "content-type": "text/html" }),
+				ok: true,
+				text: async () => "<p>Before</p><!-- This is a large HTML comment that should be removed --><p>After</p>"
+			};
+		});
+
+		const result = await (fetcher as any).fetchSingle("https://example.com/test-comment-strip");
+		expect(result).toBe("Before After");
+
+		vi.mocked(undici.fetch).mockReset();
+	});
+});
